# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccessMode

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessMode.json), input validation.

Type: ["read-only","ask","auto-review","full-access"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"full-access"
```

## AccessModeOffer

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessModeOffer.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mode | yes | [AccessMode.json](schema/AccessMode.json) |  |
| native | yes | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "mode": "read-only",
  "native": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, no emoji, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"AB"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| resetCredits | no | [QuotaResetCredits.json](schema/QuotaResetCredits.json) |  |
| usageNotice | no | object | {"properties":{"reason":{"type":"string","enum":["signed_out","unavailable"]},"message":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"observedAt":{"type":"number","minimum":0}},"required":["reason","message","observedAt"]} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"homeUnavailable":{"type":"string","enum":["home_missing","home_unreadable","foreign_home","home_conflict"]}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_in",
  "cursorSdkAuth": {
    "source": "environment",
    "status": "logged-out"
  },
  "observedAt": 4,
  "plan": "example",
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 9,
    "inputTokens": 8
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "authMethod": "unknown",
  "availability": "near_limit",
  "badgeColor": "neutral",
  "badgeUsesInitial": true,
  "blockedUntil": 8,
  "id": "example",
  "isolation": "unsupported",
  "label": "example",
  "loginRevision": "example",
  "provider": "cursor",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "unknown",
    "blockers": {
      "overflow": true
    },
    "cursorSdkAuth": {
      "source": "environment",
      "status": "logged-out"
    },
    "observedAt": 6,
    "resetCredits": {
      "available": 0
    },
    "usage": {
      "costUsd": 8
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "shortLabel": "AB"
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": true,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "source_profile",
  "mcp": "http",
  "modeSelection": true,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

### Variant 11

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "review-on-studio",
  "role": "Review-0.9-build",
  "task": "Review the 0.9 build and list failing benchmarks"
}
```

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

```json
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| runId | no | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"reasoning"` |  |
| text | yes | string |  |
| source | no | [TextSource.json](schema/TextSource.json) |  |
| summary | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "complete": false,
  "createdAt": 0,
  "id": "example",
  "raw": [],
  "text": "example",
  "type": "reasoning"
}
```

### ready

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"ready"` |  |
| documentId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| title | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |

Example:

```json
{
  "documentId": "example",
  "kind": "ready",
  "title": "example",
  "url": "example"
}
```

### saved

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"saved"` |  |
| annotation | yes | [BrowserAnnotation.json](schema/BrowserAnnotation.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "annotation": {
    "comment": "example",
    "documentId": "example",
    "element": {
      "box": {
        "height": 3,
        "width": 7,
        "x": 5,
        "y": 2
      },
      "name": "example",
      "selector": "example",
      "tag": "example",
      "text": "example",
      "xpath": "example"
    },
    "id": "example",
    "number": 2,
    "point": {
      "x": 1,
      "y": 1
    },
    "region": {
      "box": {
        "height": 4,
        "width": 2,
        "x": 8,
        "y": 9
      },
      "elements": []
    },
    "title": "example",
    "url": "example",
    "viewport": {
      "devicePixelRatio": 3,
      "height": 4,
      "scrollX": 4,
      "scrollY": 1,
      "width": 5
    }
  },
  "kind": "saved"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

## BrowserAnnotationMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserAnnotationMessage.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.annotation"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| event | yes | object | {"properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":64}},"required":["kind"],"additionalProperties":{}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": {
    "kind": "example"
  },
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "browser.annotation"
}
```

| nextNumber | no | integer | {"default":1,"minimum":1,"maximum":99} |
| markersHidden | no | boolean |  |
| bar | no | object | {"properties":{"x":{"type":"number"},"y":{"type":"number"}},"required":["x","y"]} |
| op | yes | `"configure"` |  |

```json
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |


### Variant 5

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"renumber"` |  |
| numbers | yes | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"number":{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":99}},"required":["id","number"]}} |

Example:

```json

  "numbers": [],
  "op": "renumber"
}
```

### Variant 6

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"toast"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":200} |

## CatalogList

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId, draft or workspace is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| draft | no | object | {"properties":{"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["draftId","workspaceId","provider"]} |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "catalog.list",
  "workspace": {
    "instanceId": "example",
    "provider": "antigravity",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 2,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 2,
  "type": "events"
}
```

```json
  "type": "progress"
}
```

## DelegateTaskOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegateTaskOperation.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "review-on-studio",
  "role": "Review-0.9-build",
  "task": "Review the 0.9 build and list failing benchmarks"
}
```

| openHandles | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sqlite | yes | object | {"properties":{"pageBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"walBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["pageBytes","walBytes"]} |
| activeSessions | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| streaming | no | [StreamingStorageMetrics.json](schema/StreamingStorageMetrics.json) |  |

| logs | yes | object | {"properties":{"dropped":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"failed":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"queued":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"directory":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["dropped","failed","queued"]} |

```json
  },
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| name | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| command | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## InstalledRelease

[JSON Schema](schema/InstalledRelease.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | [ReleaseVersion.json](schema/ReleaseVersion.json) |  |
| channel | yes | ["stable","preview"] |  |
| target | yes | [ReleaseTarget.json](schema/ReleaseTarget.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "channel": "preview",
  "target": "darwin-arm64",
  "version": "89823622.43078.5873-1qyhrsqjxu"
}
```

## InstallMethod

[JSON Schema](schema/InstallMethod.json), input validation.

Type: ["npm","bun","brew","script","registry"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"bun"
```

## Interaction

[JSON Schema](schema/Interaction.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [InteractionId.json](schema/InteractionId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| toolCallId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| blocking | yes | boolean |  |
| request | yes | [InteractionRequest.json](schema/InteractionRequest.json) |  |
| state | yes | [InteractionState.json](schema/InteractionState.json) |  |
| expirationReason | no | `"provider_disconnected"` |  |
| resolution | no | [InteractionResolution.json](schema/InteractionResolution.json) |  |
| resolvedBy | no | [DeviceId.json](schema/DeviceId.json) |  |
| autoReviewed | no | boolean |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| closedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "autoReviewed": false,
  "blocking": true,
  "closedAt": 0,
  "createdAt": 8,
  "expirationReason": "provider_disconnected",
  "id": "example",
  "request": {
    "kind": "question",
    "questions": []
  },
  "resolution": {
    "answers": {},
    "dismissed": false,
    "kind": "question"
  },
  "state": "pending",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## InteractionConfidence

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionConfidence.json), input validation.

Type: ["high","medium","low"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"low"
```

## InteractionEvidence

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionEvidence.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| updatesMs | yes | array | {"maxItems":2400,"items":{"type":"number","minimum":0,"maximum":10000}} |
| actionAtMs | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| cores | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| truncated | no | boolean |  |
| refreshAssumed | no | boolean |  |
| notes | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":24,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": {
    "kind": "text.type",
    "text": "example"
  },
  "cores": 7,
  "longTasks": [],
  "notes": [],
  "refreshHz": 2,
  "source": "browser-trace",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "window",
    "windowId": 2
  },
  "updatesMs": [],
  "windowMs": 4
}
```

## InteractionFilmstrip

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"image"` |  |
| mimeType | yes | `"image/jpeg"` |  |
| data | yes | string | {"maxLength":1048576} |

```json
  "data": "example",
  "mimeType": "image/jpeg",
  "type": "image"
}
```

```json
"example"
```

## InteractionMeasurement

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionMeasurement.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| settleMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| frames | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":2400} |
| effectiveFps | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| droppedFrames | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hitches | yes | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| hitchRatioMsPerS | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hitchTimeMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| verdict | yes | [InteractionVerdict.json](schema/InteractionVerdict.json) |  |
| confidence | yes | [InteractionConfidence.json](schema/InteractionConfidence.json) |  |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| notes | yes | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |
| repeat | no | [InteractionRepeat.json](schema/InteractionRepeat.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": {
    "action": "press",
    "key": "example"
  },
  "confidence": "low",
  "effectiveFps": 8,
  "filmstrip": {
    "data": "example",
    "mimeType": "image/jpeg",
    "type": "image"
  },
  "frames": 6,
  "hitches": [],
  "notes": [],
  "refreshHz": 5,
  "settleMs": 1,
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "kind": "browser-tab",
    "threadId": "example"
  },
  "verdict": "inconclusive",
  "windowMs": 5
}
```

## InteractionMetricSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionMetricSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| median | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| worst | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "median": 3,
  "worst": 9
}
```

## InteractionRepeat

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "metrics": {
    "captureOverheadPct": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 7
    },
    "effectiveFps": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 1
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 0
    },
    "hitchRatioMsPerS": {
      "median": 9,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "layoutShift": {
      "median": 1,
      "worst": 1
    },
    "longTaskMs": {
      "median": 6,
      "worst": 7
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "settleMs": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 3
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 8
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "high",
      "verdict": "minor_hitches"
    }
  ]
}
```

## InteractionRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRequest.json), input validation.

### approval

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"approval"` |  |
| target | no | [ApprovalTarget.json](schema/ApprovalTarget.json) |  |
| title | yes | string |  |
| description | no | string |  |
| options | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ApprovalOption.json"}} |
| defaultToNo | no | boolean |  |
| suppressAlwaysAllowRule | no | boolean |  |
| mcpServer | no | object | {"properties":{"name":{"type":"string"},"source":{"type":"string"}},"required":["name","source"],"additionalProperties":{}} |
| permissionUpdates | no | array | {"items":{}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "defaultToNo": true,
  "description": "example",
  "kind": "approval",
  "mcpServer": {
    "name": "example",
    "source": "example"
  },
  "options": [],
  "permissionUpdates": [],
  "target": {
    "access": "write",
    "origin": "ace",
    "tool": "example"
  },
  "title": "example"
}
```

## Item

[JSON Schema](schema/Item.json), input validation.

### html

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| runId | no | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"html"` |  |
| visualization | yes | [HtmlVisualization.json](schema/HtmlVisualization.json) |  |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "complete": true,
  "createdAt": 3,
  "executionSource": {
    "nativeSessionId": "example",
    "selection": {
      "instanceId": "example",
      "options": {},
      "provider": "acp"
    }
  },
  "id": "example",
  "nativeId": "example",
  "type": "html",
  "visualization": {
    "document": {
      "bytes": 0,
      "delivery": "inline_text_and_path",
      "height": 1,
      "kind": "image",
      "mimeType": "example",
      "name": "example",
      "sha256": "c07a534aaf527f001f4f2d50070b87ba471612b46e582e812a24936dc8f4582f",
      "width": 9
    },
    "height": 85,
    "title": "example"
  }
}
```

### message

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| runId | no | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"message"` |  |
| role | yes | ["user","assistant"] |  |
| parts | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContentPart.json"}} |
| attachments | no | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}} |
| synthetic | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| origin | no | [MessageOrigin.json](schema/MessageOrigin.json) |  |
| notAnswered | no | `"stopped"` |  |
| mergedContext | no | [MergedForkContext.json](schema/MergedForkContext.json) |  |

```json
  "createdAt": 8,

      "provider": "antigravity"
    }
  },
  "id": "example",
  "notAnswered": "stopped",
  "parts": [],
  "role": "user",
  "runId": "example",
  "type": "message"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| runId | no | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |

| nativeId | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"delegation.started"` |  |
| origin | yes | `"ace"` |  |
| childThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| title | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| role | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |
| status | yes | [ThreadStatus.json](schema/ThreadStatus.json) |  |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| generation | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | union | {"default":null,"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationOutcome.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| machine | no | object | {"properties":{"machine":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":64},"name":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["machine","name"]} |

```json
  "provider": "acp",
  "role": "example",
  "status": {
    "state": "unresponsive"
  },

}
```

### reasoning

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| wholeTurns | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

## ItemsWindowResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: itemSeqs holds the creation sequence of every item.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": 7,
  "itemsBefore": 10,
  "requestId": "example",
  "seq": 0,
  "targetSeq": null,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| title | yes | string | {"maxLength":120} |
| elapsedSeconds | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| step | no | string | {"maxLength":120} |
| result | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| truncated | no | boolean |  |
| last | no | string | {"maxLength":160} |
| needs | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WatchedRequest.json"}} |
| machine | no | string | {"maxLength":64} |
| status | yes | [ThreadStatus.json](schema/ThreadStatus.json) |  |
| requests | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/MachinePendingRequest.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "elapsedSeconds": 3,
  "requests": [],
  "state": "needs_you",
  "status": {
    "state": "limited",
    "until": 2
  },
  "threadId": "example",
  "title": "example",
  "truncated": true
}
```

| description | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| risk | yes | ["low","medium","high"] |  |
| presentation | no | `"full-access"` |  |
| default | no | boolean |  |

```json

  "label": "example",
  "risk": "low"
}
```

## ProjectIcon

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Semantic rule: Absolute HTTP or HTTPS WHATWG URL, without ASCII tabs, CR, LF or trailing whitespace; at most 4096 UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce URL parsing in application code.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"https://example.invalid/icon.png"
```

### Variant 2

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"data:image/gif;base64,Lpj6hDH70VEJUGs7+lcab52OOyxoU9K30c3n9Umu"
```

## ProviderConfiguration

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |
| maxCheckpointMb | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":256} |

Example:

```json
{
  "binaryPath": "/J",
  "enabled": true,
  "instance": "z{zzzzz}}z~",
  "maxCheckpointMb": 2,
  "provider": "cursor",
  "showOnlyFavourites": true,
  "shownGroups": []
}
```

| method | no | [InstallMethod.json](schema/InstallMethod.json) |  |
| methods | yes | array | {"maxItems":5,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InstallMethod.json"}} |
| registryPlan | no | [RegistryInstallPlan.json](schema/RegistryInstallPlan.json) |  |
| downloadOnly | no | `true` |  |
| prerequisite | no | object | {"properties":{"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"sourceUrl":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}},"required":["name","sourceUrl"]} |
| commands | yes | array | {"maxItems":4,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InstallCommand.json"}} |
| verify | no | [InstallCommand.json](schema/InstallCommand.json) |  |
| sourceUrl | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| needsAdmin | yes | boolean |  |
| versionCheckedAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| installedVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| latestVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| updateAvailable | no | boolean |  |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "uninstall",
  "commands": [],
  "message": "example",
  "method": "script",
  "methods": [],
  "needsAdmin": true,
  "prerequisite": {
    "name": "example",
    "sourceUrl": "https://example.invalid/"
  },
  "provider": "opencode",
  "sourceUrl": "https://example.invalid/",
  "status": "manual",
  "updateAvailable": false,
  "verify": {
    "args": [],
    "command": "example",
    "display": "example"
  }
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |

| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ],
  "noFinalNewline": true
}
```

```json
  "x": 8,
  "y": 8
}
```

### type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| keyCode | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":127} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "keyCode": 9,
  "kind": "key",
  "modifiers": []
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| deltaX | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| deltaY | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 9,
  "deltaY": 1,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 9,
  "y": 9
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| windowSelection | no | boolean |  |
| permissionEvents | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": false,
    "windows": false
  },
  "codecs": [
    "jpeg"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": true,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": false
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "prompt",
    "screen": "n/a"
  },
  "platform": "linux-wayland",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | string |  |
| errorCode | no | ["forbidden","permission_denied","screen_disabled","denied","target_gone","not_supported","bounds","busy","timeout","internal","target_busy","foreground_required","focus_changed","window_minimized","window_offscreen","secure_input_required","clipboard_changed","window_ambiguous","key_unsupported","modifier_unsupported","no_key_window","delivery_unconfirmed"] |  |
| holder | no | object | {"properties":{"sessionId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenId.json"},"owner":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}},"required":["sessionId","owner"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "data": null,
  "errorCode": "screen_disabled",
  "ok": false,
  "requestId": "YMq2MXOTgCKeZOOV-oojSDm6",
  "type": "screen.result"
}
```

## ScreenState

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["starting","live","stopping","stopped","failed"] |  |
| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| mode | no | [ScreenMode.json](schema/ScreenMode.json) | {"default":"background"} |
| secureInputAllowed | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| holder | no | [ScreenAgentScope.json](schema/ScreenAgentScope.json) |  |
| indicator | yes | boolean |  |
| target | yes | [ScreenTarget.json](schema/ScreenTarget.json) |  |
| permissions | yes | [ScreenPermissions.json](schema/ScreenPermissions.json) |  |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| capabilities | no | [ScreenCapabilities.json](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "capture": {
      "changeDriven": true,
      "displays": true,
      "windows": true
    },
    "codecs": [
      "h264"
    ],
    "input": {
      "keyboard": false,
      "pointer": true,
      "scroll": true,
      "text": false
    },
    "permissions": {
      "input": "prompt",
      "screen": "prompt"
    },
    "platform": "linux-wayland",
    "semanticActions": [],
    "uiTree": true,
    "version": 2
  },
  "controller": "human",
  "holder": {
    "agentId": "example",
    "threadId": "example"
  },
  "indicator": true,
  "lifecycle": "failed",
  "permissions": {
    "accessibility": false,
    "screenRecording": false
  },
  "sessionId": "Q5anAm9AJRgNXvMro2cR",
  "target": {
    "bundleIds": [
      "example"
    ],
    "displayId": 8,
    "kind": "display"
  }
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenUIActInput.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

```json
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":10,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "provider": "claude"
}
```

## SourceControlPreferences

[JSON Schema](schema/SourceControlPreferences.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Hosts are unique and the selected host must be configured

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hosts | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"host":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubHost.json"},"account":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"}},"required":["host"]}} |
| host | yes | [GitHubHost.json](schema/GitHubHost.json) |  |
| remote | yes | string | {"maxLength":100,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._-]*$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "host": "github.com",
  "hosts": [
    {
      "host": "github.com"
    }
  ],
  "remote": "origin"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/SourceControlReadiness.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| preferences | yes | [SourceControlPreferences.json](schema/SourceControlPreferences.json) |  |

| version | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":256},{"type":"null"}]} |
| accounts | yes | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"login":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"},"active":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["login","active"]}} |
| account | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| api | yes | ["ready","login_required","account_mismatch","offline","rate_limited","unavailable"] |  |
| instructions | yes | string | {"maxLength":512} |
| transport | yes | ["unchecked","ready","failed","no_remote"] |  |
| transportInstructions | yes | string | {"maxLength":512} |

```json
  "account": null,
  "accounts": [],

  "cli": "unavailable",

    "hosts": [

        "host": "github.com"
      }
    ],
    "remote": "origin"
  },
  "transport": "failed",
  "transportInstructions": "example",
  "version": null
}
```

| droppedFrames | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hitches | yes | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| hitchRatioMsPerS | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

```json
  },
```

| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"usage"},"usage":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageUsage.json"}},"required":["kind","usage"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"plan"},"plan":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StoragePlan.json"}},"required":["kind","plan"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"apply"},"apply":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageApply.json"}},"required":["kind","apply"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"compaction"},"compaction":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageCompaction.json"}},"required":["kind","compaction"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"error"},"code":{"type":"string","enum":["forbidden","busy","not_found","invalid_request","unavailable"]},"message":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["kind","code","message"]}]} |

| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}} |
